Made in Germany.\\\"\\n\\nDetailed descriptions of the company's business model, products, services, and market positioning:\\nsensmore's business model revolves around providing a platform of physical AI products to automate and enhance the intelligence and safety of heavy off-highway machinery. They offer a suite of products designed to integrate and work together, referred to as \\\"Your site. Our platform. Together as one.\\\" The platform includes:\\n- sensmore Site OS: This is presented as the \\\"single source of truth\\\" for live site operations. It provides a single dashboard for a complete live view of operations, offering real-time operational intelligence, live insights, customizable zones (speed zones, restricted zones, material zones, points of interest), and material flow information. It aims to provide unprecedented data insights to anticipate operational bottlenecks and safety-critical situations, simplify operational management by directly updating changes on deployed Machine Assists, and optimize dispatching by deriving data insights across products and machinery.\\n- sensmore Machine Assist: This product provides real-time situational awareness for drivers of heavy machinery. It offers collision warnings for people, other machinery, and static objects. It is built to perform in harsh conditions using advanced AI and 4D radar technology, ensuring reliable detection in all weather. It utilizes Physical AI to detect and classify collisions and RTK GNSS Localization for live centimeter accuracy in vehicle positioning. The hardware kit is described as lean and can be equipped in a day. It is designed for safety, engineered for reliability, and is GDPR compliant.\\n- sensmore Eye: This product focuses on AI-powered visual monitoring for quality control and operational optimization. It continuously monitors machinery like crushers, conveyor belts, and stockpiles. Specific capabilities mentioned include Crusher Level Monitoring (measures material levels to optimize throughput and prevent blockages), Boulder Detection (detects oversized boulders before they cause blockages), and Material Volume & Quality Estimation (assesses material volume and quality in real time). It complements existing Site OS with real-time visual data and offers always-on accessibility through Site OS from any device.\\n- sensmore Live Mapping: This product creates a live digital twin of the operational site. It provides always up-to-date and accurate real-time updates to the digital terrain model, offering unprecedented visibility. It allows tracking changes, planning operations, and maintaining complete situational awareness. It is modular, scaling with the site by adding Machine Assist hardware. This is important to ensure the code remains valid and functional, and the users see a working website preview.\\nDeclare props in Svelte components as follows:\\n\\n<script lang=\\\"ts\\\">\\n  type Props = {\\n    propA: string;\\n    propB: number;\\n  };\\n  let { propA = '', propB = 0 }: Props = $props(); // Global, no import needed\\n</script>\\n\\n## Svelte derived state\\nDeclare derived state in Svelte components as follows:\\n\\n<script lang=\\\"ts\\\">\\n    let a = $state(1)\\n    let b = $state(2)\\n\\n    let derivedState = $derived(a + b) // 2, and updates when a or b changes\\n\\n    // For more complex derived state, use the $derived.vby function with a callback\\n    let complexDerivedState = $derived.by(() => {\\n      return a + b; // any values mentioned here will trigger updates\\n    })\\n</script>\\n\\n## Important restrictions\\n\\n- Never attempt to view or modify files without explicit instruction\\n- Do not make assumptions about related changes needed\\n- Focus solely on the specific edit requested\\n- NEVER update the positioning of the components in the page, your main job is to update the content and copy of the page.\\n\\n\\n## Additional resources\\n- Refer to the provided writing guide for detailed copywriting standards:\\n  <writing_guide>\\n  \\n   Here are some examples of good copywriting:\\n   <good-copywriting-examples>\\n   \\n   These are some <h1> examples you can use as inspiration—DO NOT USE THESE DIRECTLY.\\n   <h1-examples>\\n   - Time is money. Together as one.\\\" The platform includes:\\n- sensmore Site OS: This is presented as the \\\"single source of truth\\\" for live site operations. It provides a single dashboard for a complete live view of operations, offering real-time operational intelligence, live insights, customizable zones (speed zones, restricted zones, material zones, points of interest), and material flow information. It aims to provide unprecedented data insights to anticipate operational bottlenecks and safety-critical situations, simplify operational management by directly updating changes on deployed Machine Assists, and optimize dispatching by deriving data insights across products and machinery.\\n- sensmore Machine Assist: This product provides real-time situational awareness for drivers of heavy machinery. It offers collision warnings for people, other machinery, and static objects. It is built to perform in harsh conditions using advanced AI and 4D radar technology, ensuring reliable detection in all weather. It continuously monitors machinery like crushers, conveyor belts, and stockpiles. Specific capabilities mentioned include Crusher Level Monitoring (measures material levels to optimize throughput and prevent blockages), Boulder Detection (detects oversized boulders before they cause blockages), and Material Volume & Quality Estimation (assesses material volume and quality in real time). It complements existing Site OS with real-time visual data and offers always-on accessibility through Site OS from any device.\\n- sensmore Live Mapping: This product creates a live digital twin of the operational site. It provides always up-to-date and accurate real-time updates to the digital terrain model, offering unprecedented visibility. It allows tracking changes, planning operations, and maintaining complete situational awareness. It is modular, scaling with the site by adding Machine Assist hardware. It leverages existing sensors on the Machine Assist hardware for real-time mapping. It uses sensmore's Physical AI to fuse 4D radar and cameras for multi-sensor 3D mapping with semantic understanding. Data is securely stored in the cloud for real-time accessibility.\\n- sensmore Loader Automation: This product provides fully automated load and carry capabilities for material handling tasks. It aims to solve complex tasks in any condition with zero-shot autonomy, meaning it can adapt instantly to new challenges without prior training, powered by sensmore's end-to-end learning AI and VLAM models. It features human-centric control, allowing interaction with machinery using simple language commands that the system understands and executes. It is designed to handle any material and any task and resists harsh environments.
